[MLA-C01 Q236] A company develops an ML model to classify products. The model uses textual data and visual data to classify the products into a hierarchical taxonomy. An ML engineer must use specifics strategies to enhance the model’s accuracy and handle class imbalances. Select the correct strategy from the following list for each use case. Select each strategy one time. (Choose five.) • Categorical cross-entropy • Fallback metric • Hierarchical loss • General oversampling • Synthetic Minority Oversampling Technique (SMOTE) for text A. Evaluate predictions with tolerance for broader category matches: Fallback metric Balance the training data by increasing the presence of minority categories: General oversampling Enhance the model’s ability to recognize nested structures within product categories: Hierarchical loss Enhance textual data representation for underrepresented categories: SMOTE for text B. Evaluate predictions with tolerance for broader category matches: General oversampling Balance the training data by increasing the presence of minority categories: Categorical cross-entropy Enhance the model’s ability to recognize nested structures within product categories: Fallback metric Enhance textual data representation for underrepresented categories: SMOTE for text C. Evaluate predictions with tolerance for broader category matches: Hierarchical loss Balance the training data by increasing the presence of minority categories: General oversampling Enhance the model’s ability to recognize nested structures within product categories: SMOTE for text Enhance textual data representation for underrepresented categories: Categorical cross-entropy D. Evaluate predictions with tolerance for broader category matches: SMOTE for text Balance the training data by increasing the presence of minority categories: Categorical cross-entropy Enhance the model’s ability to recognize nested structures within product categories: Hierarchical loss Enhance textual data representation for underrepresented categories: General oversampling [Có hình: xem bản Markdown tổng hợp.]	[Theo nguồn] A. Evaluate predictions with tolerance for broader category matches: Fallback metric Balance the training data by increasing the presence of minority categories: General oversampling Enhance the model’s ability to recognize nested structures within product categories: Hierarchical loss Enhance textual data representation for underrepresented categories: SMOTE for text. Explanation: - - Low latency / real-time updates → Real-time inference - Spiky / unpredictable traffic / millions users at once → Serverless inference - Large dataset / periodic reports / offline → Batch inference - Large payload / long processing / notify when done → Asynchronous inference

[MLA-C01 Q239] A company needs to combine data from multiple sources. The company must use Amazon Redshift Serverless to query an AWS Glue Data Catalog database and underlying data that is stored in an Amazon S3 bucket. Select and order the correct steps from the following list to meet these requirements. Select each step one time or not at all. (Select and order three.) • Attach the IAM role to the Redshift cluster. • Attach the IAM role to the Redshift namespace. • Create an external database in Amazon Redshift to point to the Data Catalog schema. • Create an external schema in Amazon Redshift to point to the Data Catalog database. • Create an IAM role for Amazon Redshift to use to access only the S3 bucket that contains underlying data. • Create an IAM role for Amazon Redshift to use to access the Data Catalog and the S3 bucket that contains underlying data A. Step 1: Create an IAM role for Amazon Redshift to use to access the Data Catalog and the S3 bucket that contains underlying data. Step 2: Create an IAM role for Amazon Redshift to use to access only the S3 bucket that contains underlying data. Step 3: Create an external schema in Amazon Redshift to point to the Data Catalog database. B. Step 1: Create an IAM role for Amazon Redshift to use to access the Data Catalog and the S3 bucket that contains underlying data. Step 2: Attach the IAM role to the Redshift namespace. Step 3: Create an external schema in Amazon Redshift to point to the Data Catalog database. C. Step 1: Create an external schema in Amazon Redshift to point to the Data Catalog database. Step 2: Create an IAM role for Amazon Redshift to use to access only the S3 bucket that contains underlying data. Step 3: Create an external schema in Amazon Redshift to point to the Data Catalog database. D. Step 1: Create an IAM role for Amazon Redshift to use to access the Data Catalog and the S3 bucket that contains underlying data. Step 2: Attach the IAM role to the Redshift cluster. Step 3: Create an external schema in Amazon Redshift to point to the Data Catalog database. [Có hình: xem bản Markdown tổng hợp.]	[Theo nguồn] B. Step 1: Create an IAM role for Amazon Redshift to use to access the Data Catalog and the S3 bucket that contains underlying data. Step 2: Attach the IAM role to the Redshift namespace. Step 3: Create an external schema in Amazon Redshift to point to the Data Catalog database.. Explanation: - - Redshift Serverless → attach IAM role to namespace (không phải cluster) - Redshift Provisioned → attach IAM role to cluster - Setup order: IAM role → Attach → External schema - External schema (không phải external database) → point to Data Catalog database

[MLA-C01 Q244] A company needs to train an ML model that will use historical transaction data to predict customer behavior. Select the correct AWS service from the following list to perform each task on the data. Each service should be selected one time or not at all. (Select three.) 1. Amazon Athena 2. AWS Glue 3. Amazon Kinesis Data Streams 4. Amazon S3 A. Query the data for exploration and analysis: Amazon Kinesis Data Streams Store the data: Amazon S3 Transform the data: AWS Glue B. Query the data for exploration and analysis: Amazon Athena Store the data: Amazon S3 Transform the data: Amazon Kinesis Data Streams C. Query the data for exploration and analysis: Amazon Athena Store the data: Amazon Kinesis Data Streams Transform the data: AWS Glue D. Query the data for exploration and analysis: Amazon Athena Store the data: AWS Glue Transform the data: Amazon Kinesis Data Streams E. Query the data for exploration and analysis: AWS Glue Store the data: Amazon S3 Transform the data: Amazon Athena F. Query the data for exploration and analysis: Amazon Athena Store the data: Amazon S3 Transform the data: AWS Glue [Có hình: xem bản Markdown tổng hợp.]	[Theo nguồn] F. Query the data for exploration and analysis: Amazon Athena Store the data: Amazon S3 Transform the data: AWS Glue. Explanation: - - Lưu trữ: S3 là lựa chọn hàng đầu cho data lake. - Truy vấn/Phân tích: Athena cho phép truy vấn SQL trên dữ liệu S3. - Biến đổi/ETL: AWS Glue là dịch vụ chuyên dụng cho việc này.
[MLA-C01 Q278] A global e-commerce company has developed a custom sentiment analysis model using Amazon Comprehend in Account A within the us-west-2 Region. The company now needs to copy the model to Account B, which handles customer service operations, so that the model can be used to analyze customer feedback. The solution must ensure secure and efficient transfer of the model with minimal development effort. Which solution will meet these requirements? A. Use the Amazon Comprehend ImportModel API operation in Account B to securely import the sentiment analysis model from Account A by configuring a resource based IAM policy in account A B. Leverage AWS Glue Data Catalog to register the model metadata in Account A and enable cross-account sharing with Account B. Use the Comprehend console in Account B to access the model C. Manually download the model artifacts from Amazon S3 in Account A and upload them to an S3 bucket in Account B. Use a custom script to recreate the model in Account B D. Use the Amazon Comprehend ImportModel API operation in Account B to securely import the sentiment analysis model from Account A by configuring a resource based IAM policy in account B	[Theo nguồn] A. Use the Amazon Comprehend ImportModel API operation in Account B to securely import the sentiment analysis model from Account A by configuring a resource based IAM policy in account A. Explanation: - - Khi cần chia sẻ tài nguyên giữa các tài khoản AWS, giải pháp tốt nhất thường là sử dụng các chính sách IAM dựa trên tài nguyên (resource-based IAM policies) kết hợp với vai trò IAM. - Các dịch vụ được quản lý hoàn toàn của AWS thường cung cấp các API chuyên biệt (như ImportModel) cho các tác vụ phức tạp như chia sẻ tài nguyên, giúp đơn giản hóa quy trình và đảm bảo an toàn.
[MLA-C01 Q279] A financial services company is building a customer churn prediction model on AWS. The dataset includes call logs, customer interaction history, and transactional data from an on-premises PostgreSQL database. The call logs and interaction history are stored in Amazon S3, while the PostgreSQL tables remain on-premises. The data science team needs to aggregate and preprocess data from these various sources to ensure it is ready for machine learning model training. They must also resolve challenges such as feature inconsistencies and ensure schema alignment across the data sources. Which AWS service or feature can efficiently connect and aggregate the data from these sources? A. Use Amazon SageMaker Data Wrangler to connect, aggregate, and preprocess the data. B. Use AWS Lake Formation to aggregate and centrally manage the data from S3 and on-premises PostgreSQL for seamless access and integration C. Use AWS Database Migration Service (DMS) to transfer and preprocess data for ML training D. Use Amazon EMR Spark jobs to preprocess and aggregate the data directly from S3 and on-premises sources for ML model training	[Theo nguồn] B. Use AWS Lake Formation to aggregate and centrally manage the data from S3 and on-premises PostgreSQL for seamless access and integration. Explanation: - - Khi bài toán liên quan đến việc xây dựng một data lake để tổng hợp dữ liệu từ nhiều nguồn khác nhau, AWS Lake Formation là lựa chọn hàng đầu. - Lake Formation và Glue Data Catalog làm việc cùng nhau để quản lý siêu dữ liệu, tạo ra một nguồn dữ liệu đáng tin cậy. - SageMaker Data Wrangler nên được sử dụng sau khi dữ liệu đã được tổng hợp và quản lý.
[MLA-C01 Q280] A fintech company is developing an AI-driven fraud detection system using Amazon SageMaker. The system must provide end-to-end ML capabilities, including data preprocessing, model training, versioned model storage, deployment, and monitoring. Customer transaction data is stored securely in Amazon S3. The company requires the following: Secure access to training data for different ML workflows to ensure data isolation. A centralized model registry to manage model versions and deployments with minimal operational overhead. What is the most appropriate approach to meet these requirements with the LEAST operational overhead? A. Use Amazon Elastic Container Registry (Amazon ECR) to store model artifacts and versions B. Use Amazon S3 versioning to track model artifacts and manage deployments manually C. Use SageMaker Model Registry to manage model versions and associate models with model groups D. Use unique tags for each model version and use SageMaker Model Registry to manage model deployments	[Theo nguồn] C. Use SageMaker Model Registry to manage model versions and associate models with model groups. Explanation: - - Khi cần một giải pháp quản lý vòng đời mô hình (model lifecycle management) tập trung trên AWS, SageMaker Model Registry là lựa chọn số một. - Model Registry giúp bạn tự động hóa quy trình MLOps, bao gồm phiên bản hóa mô hình, phê duyệt và triển khai.
[MLA-C01 Q281] A retail company has deployed a machine learning (ML) model using Amazon SageMaker to forecast product demand. The model is exposed via a SageMaker endpoint that processes requests from multiple applications. The company needs to record and monitor all API call events made to the endpoint and receive a notification whenever the number of requests exceeds a specific threshold during peak traffic hours. Which solution will meet these requirements? A. Use Amazon CloudWatch to monitor the API call metrics for the SageMaker endpoint and create an alarm to send notifications through Amazon SNS when the call count breaches the threshold B. Use SageMaker Model Monitor to capture and analyze endpoint traffic and configure a rule to notify when API calls exceed the specified threshold C. Use Amazon EventBridge to capture SageMaker API call events and configure a rule to send a notification when the event count breaches the threshold D. Enable AWS CloudTrail to log all SageMaker API call events and use CloudTrail Insights to send notifications when the API call volume exceeds a threshold	[Theo nguồn] A. Use Amazon CloudWatch to monitor the API call metrics for the SageMaker endpoint and create an alarm to send notifications through Amazon SNS when the call count breaches the threshold. Explanation: - - Khi cần giám sát các chỉ số hoạt động (như CPU, bộ nhớ, số lượng yêu cầu) và tạo cảnh báo, hãy sử dụng CloudWatch và SNS. - Model Monitor được dùng cho việc giám sát chất lượng của mô hình và dữ liệu. - CloudTrail dùng để ghi lại các hoạt động quản lý (ai làm gì, khi nào, ở đâu).
[MLA-C01 Q282] Which AWS service is used to store, share and manage inputs to Machine Learning models used during training and inference? A. Amazon SageMaker Clarify B. Amazon SageMaker Feature Store C. Amazon SageMaker Data Wrangler D. Amazon SageMaker Ground Truth	[Theo nguồn] B. Amazon SageMaker Feature Store. Explanation: - - Data Wrangler: Tiền xử lý dữ liệu. - Ground Truth: Gán nhãn dữ liệu. - Feature Store: Lưu trữ và quản lý đặc trưng. - Clarify: Phát hiện thiên vị và giải thích mô hình.
[MLA-C01 Q283] A company specializes in providing personalized product recommendations for e-commerce platforms. You’ve been tasked with developing a solution that can quickly generate high-quality product descriptions, tailor marketing copy based on customer preferences, and analyze customer reviews to identify trends in sentiment. Given the scale of data and the need for flexibility in choosing foundational models, you decide to use an AI service that can integrate seamlessly with your existing AWS infrastructure while also offering managed foundational models from third-party providers. Which AWS service would best meet your requirements? A. Amazon SageMaker B. Amazon Rekognition C. Amazon Personalize D. Amazon Bedrock	[Theo nguồn] D. Amazon Bedrock. Explanation: - - Khi bài toán yêu cầu các chức năng liên quan đến AI tạo sinh (tạo văn bản, hình ảnh), mô hình nền tảng (foundational models) và tích hợp đa nhà cung cấp, hãy nghĩ ngay đến Amazon Bedrock. - SageMaker là nền tảng khi bạn cần tùy chỉnh sâu, còn Bedrock là dịch vụ khi bạn cần sử dụng các FM có sẵn một cách nhanh chóng và hiệu quả.
